SELECT [c].[Name] AS [CourseName], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Sessions] AS [s]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = [s].[CourseId]) AS [TotalSessions], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Sessions] AS [s0]
    INNER JOIN [AttendanceRecords] AS [a] ON [s0].[Id] = [a].[SessionId]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = [s0].[CourseId]) AS [TotalAttendance], CASE
    WHEN (
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM [Sessions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [c].[Id] = [s1].[CourseId]) = 0 THEN 0.0E0
    ELSE ROUND((CAST((
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM [Sessions] AS [s2]
        INNER JOIN [AttendanceRecords] AS [a0] ON [s2].[Id] = [a0].[SessionId]
        WHERE [c].[Id] = [s2].[CourseId]) AS float) / CAST((
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM [Sessions] AS [s3]
        WHERE [c].[Id] = [s3].[CourseId]) * @__students_Count_0 AS float)) * 100.0E0, 1)
END AS [AttendanceRate]
FROM [Courses] AS [c]

    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Sessions] AS [s]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = [s].[CourseId]) AS [TotalSessions], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Sessions] AS [s0]
    INNER JOIN [AttendanceRecords] AS [a] ON [s0].[Id] = [a].[SessionId]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = [s0].[CourseId]) AS [TotalAttendance], CASE
    WHEN (
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM [Sessions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [c].[Id] = [s1].[CourseId]) = 0 THEN 0.0E0
    ELSE ROUND((CAST((
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM [Sessions] AS [s2]
        INNER JOIN [AttendanceRecords] AS [a0] ON [s2].[Id] = [a0].[SessionId]
        WHERE [c].[Id] = [s2].[CourseId]) AS float) / CAST((
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        FROM [Sessions] AS [s3]
        WHERE [c].[Id] = [s3].[CourseId]) * @__students_Count_0 AS float)) * 100.0E0, 1)
END AS [AttendanceRate]
FROM [Courses] AS [c]
